| `bad_args` | the request has a key other than `id`, `op` and `args`, or its args fail the checks above (an unknown key, a wrong type, too many files, a name too long, a non `Uint8Array` file, an `open` of the other kind, an import `open` the engine cannot import in this frame, an import's `sheets` or `project` in a `pcb` frame, one without the other, or an `open` that is not one of the `sheets`, a `sheet.enter` `file` that no sheet at its `path` holds, a `select` list of another shape, a `place` `value` or `footprint` that is empty or over 200 characters or a footprint's `footprint`, a `project.write` with no files, a path not in its normal form, a text that is not a string, more than 32 MiB of text, or a path that names a folder or runs through a file, a `project.libs` list of another shape or a nickname the island serves another way, a `footprint.models` uuid or model of another shape, `project.plot` args of another shape, `symbol.get`, `symbol.set` or `sheet.annotate` args of another shape, a `keys.claim` of another shape, a `symbol.set` the symbol itself bounds (`message` says which), the args of a board op of another shape, a `board.item.set` of another kind's key or one the item or the engine refuses (`message` says why when the item does), args on an op that takes none, the args of a sim op of another shape, a type key KiCad's tables lack, a net or source the netlist does not hold, an FFT signal the transient lacks, a vector name the run lacks, a model file path the engine refuses, `project.export` args of another shape or settings the engine refuses (`message` "the engine refused the settings"), `board.rules` args of another shape). `symbols.set`, the exchanges, the edits and `settings.set` also answer it when the engine refuses the args at once ("the engine refused the set", "... the exchange", "... the edit", "... the setting"), and `settings.set` when the section does not take the value (`message` the engine's). |
| `island_error` | the frame failed while handling the request (for example the file system refused to empty the project folder, `sheet.tree` or `layers.get` got no answer from the engine within 30 s, the engine refused the text `place` gave it, or a `project.plot` wrote more than the protocol carries, or a `sheet.check` record said its report was not written or the report was of another shape, or a `board.check`, `board.preview` or `board.update` report was of another shape, or a dialog came up during a `board.check` (`message` its title), or `board.item.get` or `board.explain` got an answer of another shape from the engine, or a sim op did, or `sim.vectors` asked more than 256 MiB of numbers, or a `project.export` wrote more than the protocol carries, a folder inside a folder or a link, or a dialog came up during it (`message` its title), or `board.rules` got an answer of another shape); `message` carries the failure. The next request is still handled. |
| `not_ready` | the engine has not booted yet (before `ev.ready`; `project.write` and `project.libs` too), or `project.save`, `place`, `select`, `footprint.models`, `project.plot`, `project.export`, `symbol.get`, `symbol.set`, `sheet.annotate`, `sheet.check`, a board op (`board.rules` included) or a sim op with no successfully opened document, or any request but `shutdown` and the library reads after an import stopped the engine (`message` "the engine stopped"; the host reloads the frame). |
| `not_found` | `place`: the mirror has no such library, or the library no such item; `message` is `lib:name`. `project.libs`: a nickname that is no project library of the folder (no `KiCad` row of a `${KIPRJMOD}` path in its tables, or its file or folder missing); `message` is the nickname. `footprint.models`: no footprint of the board has the uuid; `message` is the uuid. `symbol.get` and `symbol.set`: no sheet holds a symbol of that uuid (`message` the uuid), or, for `symbol.set`, the asked sheet path does not (`message` "<uuid> on <sheetPath>"). `board.explain`: no mark of the board has the uuid. `board.item.set`: the board holds no item of the uuid; `message` is the uuid. `symedit.items.set` and `symedit.items.remove`: the open symbol holds no item of a uuid; `message` is the first. `symbol.forEditor`: no sheet holds the symbol (`message` the uuid); `symbol.push`: the path does not hold it (`message` "<uuid> on <sheetPath>"). `sim.model.get` and `sim.model.set`: no sheet holds the symbol, or, for a set, the asked path does not (`message` "<uuid> on <sheetPath>"). `board.rules`: the board holds neither item, or not both; `message` the uuids asked. `symbols.set`: an instance asked is not in the design (`message` "<uuid> on <sheetPath>", or how many are missing). |
| `open_document` | `project.write`: a file of the document kind the frame holds (any `.kicad_sch` in a `sch` frame, any `.kicad_pcb` in a `pcb` frame) outside `imports/` and outside a design block's own folder (`<lib>.kicad_blocks/<name>.kicad_block/`); `message` names the first such path, and nothing is written. |
| `nothing_to_open` | `project.open` named no file and `files` holds none of the frame's kind. |
| `open_failed` | the file to open or import was not written (dropped, or absent from `files`), or KiCad's load did not settle within the frame's time limit, or an import's sheet or project file was not written (`message` names it), or a schematic import's open of the empty sheet at its target did not settle (`message` names the target), or an import's converted file would be no valid path (over 255 bytes). |
| `import_failed` | `project.import`: the engine refused the file, a dialog other than the importer's own came up (`message` names it), KiCad's error box came up (`message` carries its text), the layer mapping was refused, the converted board's save wrote nothing or the converted schematic's save did not write its root, or the import passed its time limit; the dialogs were closed first. Or the engine stopped inside the import (its call trapped): `message` is "the engine stopped: " and the engine's error, answered at once with no dialog touched; no `ev.state` `fatal` follows, and later requests answer `not_ready` (`project.import` above). |
| `unsupported` | the engine build lacks the export the op needs, or the export answers nothing in this frame's kind (`sheet.tree` in a `pcb` frame, `layers.get` in a `sch` frame), or `project.import` without the frame's import export (`kicadOpenFile` and `kicadSaveBoard` in a `pcb` frame, `kicadImportSchematic` in a `sch` frame; an island older than `v0.2.3-cc8` answers `project.import needs a pcb frame` in a `sch` frame), or `place` of a symbol in a `pcb` frame or a footprint in a `sch` frame, or `select` without its exports, or `project.libs` without `kicadLibsAddEntry`, `kicadLibsReload` or `kicadCollabBusy`, or `footprint.models` in a `sch` frame or without `kicadFootprintSetModels`, `kicadCollabTestItemBlob` or `kicadCollabBusy`, or `project.plot` in a `sch` frame or without `kicadPlotBoard` or `kicadCollabBusy`, or `project.export` of a job of the other frame's kind or without the frame's export (`kicadExportBoardOutput`, `kicadExportSchematicOutput`) or `kicadCollabBusy`, or `board.rules` in a `sch` frame or without `kicadResolveRules`, or `symbol.get`, `symbol.set`, `sheet.annotate` or `sheet.check` in a `pcb` frame or without their exports (`kicadSymbolGet`, `kicadSymbolSet`, `kicadAnnotate`, `kicadCheckSheet`, `kicadCollabBusy`), or a board op (`board.preview`, `board.update`, `board.check`, `board.explain`, `board.item.get`, `board.item.set`) in a `sch` frame or without its exports (`kicadUpdateBoardFromSchematic`, `kicadCheckBoard`, `kicadExplainViolation`, `kicadBoardItemGet`, `kicadBoardItemSet`, `kicadCollabBusy`), or a `symedit` op in a `sch` or a `pcb` frame or without its exports, or a document op in a `symedit` frame (The symbol editor, above), or `symbol.convert` in a `pcb` frame, or `symbol.forEditor`, `symbol.push` or `lib.update` outside a `sch` frame or without their exports, or a sim op outside a `sch` frame, without its exports, or (a run, `sim.result`, `sim.vectors`, `sim.stop`, `sim.release`) where the release serves no simulator service (`message` `ngspice_service.wasm`); `message` names the export, or says why. `settings.get` or `settings.set` of a section the frame does not hold, and a `pcb` frame's `variants` set. |
| `not_applied` | `chrome.show`, `readonly`, `sheet.enter`, `layers.visible` or `layers.active`: the engine answered anything but success, or did not answer within 30 s. `select`: the editor refused the clear, or did not hold the selection within about 2 s. `footprint.models`: the engine refused the list, or the footprint's model lines did not read back as sent within about 5 s. `project.plot`: the engine refused the plot, or its record did not come within 120 s. `project.export`: its record did not come within 45 s. `symbol.set`: the engine refused the set, or the symbol did not read back with the change within about 5 s. `sheet.annotate` and `sheet.check`: the engine refused the run, or its record did not come within 60 s. `board.check`: the engine refused the run, or its record did not come within 60 s; `board.preview` and `board.update` within 30 s. `board.item.set`: the item did not read back with the change within about 5 s. A `symedit` change, an open or a new symbol, `symedit.markSaved` and `symbol.push`: the engine did not show it within about 5 s (`symedit.svg` 30 s, `lib.update` 30 s); `select` in a `symedit` frame: the engine refused the selection. `sim.model.set`: the model did not read back within about 5 s. `sim.run`: the engine refused the run, or it was neither accepted nor ended within about 60 s (the island then stops it). `sim.opOnSheet`: the engine refused it or its apply queue did not empty within about 5 s. `symbols.set`, `symbols.exchange`, `footprints.exchange`, `sch.edit`, `board.edit` and `settings.set`: the record did not come within 30 s. |
| `busy` | `project.open`, `project.import` or `project.save` while the engine is still loading a file, or `key.press`, `view.fit`, `project.import` or a `sheet.enter` with a `file` that must press Next Sheet while a popup menu or a dialog is up, or a schematic's `project.save` while a dialog or a menu bar popup is up, or any request but `shutdown` and the library reads while a `project.import` runs, or `place` while a load is parked, a popup menu or a dialog is up, or the editor does not take the item, or `select`, `footprint.models`, `project.plot`, `project.export`, `symbol.get`, `symbol.set`, `sheet.annotate` or `sheet.check` while a load is parked, a popup menu or a dialog is up, or a placed item hangs off the pointer, or `project.libs` while a load is parked, a popup menu or a dialog is up, or any request but `symbol.get`, `sheet.tree`, `layers.get`, `board.item.get`, `board.explain`, `board.rules`, `shutdown` and the library reads while a `sheet.check` or a `board.check` runs, or a board op while a load is parked, a popup menu or a dialog is up, or a placed item hangs off the pointer, or `sim.model.set`, `sim.netlist`, `sim.run` or `sim.opOnSheet` while a load is parked, a popup menu or a dialog is up or a placed item hangs off the pointer, or `sim.run`, `sim.netlist`, `sim.vectors` or `sim.release` while a run is live, or `sim.result` of the live run; nothing was changed, and the host may send the request again later. The one exception is a `sheet.enter` with a `file` that answers `busy` partway through its walk: `path` was entered, so the editor shows the first sheet at `path` or a sheet the walk passed (`sheet.tree` `currentFile` names it), and the host may send the request again later. `symbols.set`, the exchanges, the edits and `settings.set` while a `sheet.check` or `board.check` runs (`symbols.get`, `board.footprints` and `settings.get` are answered). |
| `plot_failed` | `project.plot`: the Gerber job ended with any code but success, or the drill job asked for did; `message` is the plot's first `error` line (the frame's paths taken out), else the job and its code. Nothing is answered of a failed plot's files. `symedit.svg`: KiCad wrote no SVG. |
| `no_schematic` | `board.preview` and `board.update`: no schematic sits beside the opened board with its stem; `message` names the path expected. |
| `schematic_unreadable` | `board.preview` and `board.update`: the board engine could not read the schematic, or a dialog came up during the run (`message` its title). |
| `stale` | `board.update`: the board or the schematic changed since the preview that gave the token; nothing changed. |
| `not_annotated` | `board.update`: parts of the schematic are not numbered yet, or numbered twice; nothing changed. |
| `rules_invalid` | `board.check`, `board.explain` and `board.rules`: the board's custom rules file does not compile; `message` is KiCad's parse message for `board.check`. `settings.set` of `customRules` (not a dry run): the rules do not compile; nothing is written, `message` the first error. |
| `no_rule` | `board.explain`: KiCad names no single rule for the mark's rule key; `message` the mark's uuid. `board.rules`: KiCad applies no rule to the item or between the two; `message` the uuids asked. |
| `export_failed` | `project.export`: the run ended with any code but success; `message` is its first `error` line, else for the engine's own refusal its last line, else "the export ended with code N" (the frame's paths taken out). Nothing is answered of a failed run's files. |
| `no_variant` | `symbols.get` or `symbols.set` with a variant the design does not name (or one deleted between the check and the apply); `message` the variant. Nothing changed. |
| `duplicate_designator` | `board.edit` `reannotate`, a preview included: two footprints would end with one designator; nothing changed, `message` names them (KiCad's own lines). |
| `held_edits` | `symedit.open` or `symedit.new` without `discard` while the open symbol holds changes; nothing changed. |
| `no_symbol` | a `symedit` op that reads or changes the symbol (every one but `symedit.open`, `symedit.new` and `symedit.state`), or `select` in a `symedit` frame, while no symbol is open. |
| `unreadable` | `symbol.convert`: no reader of KiCad's takes the file, or the reader refused it; `message` is KiCad's sentence. |
| `too_large` | `symbol.convert`: the file holds more than 2,000 symbols or 4 MiB of their text. |
| `no_directive` | `sim.run` with `{ kind: "sheet" }`: the schematic carries no simulation directive. |
| `no_simulation` | `sim.result` or `sim.vectors` of a run the service no longer holds (a later run of its kind, the previous document, a released or dead service, or no such run), or an FFT `of` a run that is not the latest transient. |
| `sim_failed` | `sim.run` that ngspice could not run before its acceptance, or the end event of a run ngspice ran and that made no plot; `message` is ngspice's first error line. |
| `circuit` | `sim.run`: the circuit could not be read or its netlist made (a part not numbered, a model KiCad could not make, a netlist error); `sim.netlist` answers its problems. |
| `service_failed` | `sim.run` whose service could not be made, and the end event of a run whose service died. |
| `model_invalid` | `sim.model.set`: the engine refused the model (a type, a parameter, a value, the pin map or the file); nothing changed. |
| `save_failed` | `project.save`: the engine wrote nothing (in a `sch` frame, no file arrived within about 8 s; in a `pcb` frame, also when the board save gave no answer within 30 s). |
| `no_sheet` | a `dsedit` op that reads or changes the sheet (every one but `dsedit.open` and `dsedit.state`), or `select` in a `ds` frame, while no drawing sheet is open. <!-- ext:ds-i1 --> |
| `no_footprint` | an `fpedit` op that reads or changes the footprint (every one but `fpedit.open`, `fpedit.new` and `fpedit.state`), or `select` in an `fpedit` frame, while no footprint is open. <!-- ext:fp-i1 --> |
| `not_a_drawing` | `graphics.import`: the file is neither a DXF (a `SECTION` head) nor an SVG (an `svg` root element) by its content. <!-- ext:fp-i5 --> |
| `block_failed` | a design block op KiCad refused or could not finish; nothing changed. `block.save`: no sheet at that path, a sheet holding sheets, nothing selected, no single selected group linked to a block. `block.applyLayout`: no group asked is linked to a block whose row is loaded, a uuid that names no group of the board, or a window of KiCad's met during the run (`message` its title). `message` is KiCad's first line where KiCad gave one. <!-- ext:i-block-layout --> |
| `held` | `action.run` of an action the island holds in this frame under a cap `ev.ready` listed: nothing ran, the host opens its own door for it (as for `ev.held`); `message` is the action. <!-- ext:m1-i --> |
| `unknown_action` | `action.run` of a name that is not a tool action of this frame (the engine found none): nothing ran; `message` is the name. <!-- ext:m1-i --> |
| `no_item` | `contextMenu.choose` or `contextMenu.highlight` of an id the parked menu was not sent with (an omitted action, an unknown id), or of a submenu or a separator. <!-- ext:m1-i --> |
| `disabled` | `contextMenu.choose` or `contextMenu.highlight` of an item sent disabled, or one the engine finds disabled now. <!-- ext:m1-i --> |
| `bus_label_unknown` | `buses.migration.apply`: a uuid of the choices is not a label the schematic holds; nothing changed, no undo step. <!-- ext:m10-i --> |
| `no_picker` | `prompt.pick` of a kind the prompt's dialog has no picker for (only the placement asks have pickers): nothing ran; `message` names the ask and the kind. <!-- ext:m1x-i --> |
| `no_tuning` | `board.tuning.get` or `board.tuning.set` while no tuning tool of KiCad's runs (or it ended before a set applied); nothing changed. <!-- ext:m4-i --> |
| `sheet_file` | `sch.item.set`: KiCad refused a sheet's file change (`message` its word: `args`, `normalize`, `case_clash`, `in_use`, `exists`, `copy`, `save`, `load` or `recursion`); nothing changed. <!-- ext:m5-i --> |
| `no_match` | `find.replace`: nothing was replaced (no target matched any more); the schematic as it was, no undo step. <!-- ext:st-find-e-i --> |
| `taken` | `keys.editor.set`: a key is a chord the island seeds for another tool of the pencil case (`theme/pencil-tools.json`); `message` names the key and that tool's action. Nothing was sent to the engine. <!-- ext:st-keys-i --> |
| `too_few` | `board.multichannel` `generate`: KiCad finds one or no sheet, group or component class to make a placement rule area for; `message` is KiCad's sentence; nothing changed. <!-- ext:st-mc-i --> |
| `copy_failed` | `board.multichannel` `repeat`: KiCad's Repeat Layout could not copy onto a target; the whole commit reverted, nothing changed; `message` is KiCad's line. <!-- ext:st-mc-i --> |
| `session_invalid` | `board.session`: KiCad could not read the router session (a section missing, a part reference the board lacks, a via padstack the session does not define, or a parse error); `message` is KiCad's first line with no folder of the frame named; nothing changed. <!-- ext:st-ses-i --> |
| `theme_frame` | `theme.set` in a frame whose editor the engine's color switch does not reach (on engine build T18b every frame but `sch` and `pcb`): nothing changes and nothing is kept; that frame boots in the kept colors the next time it opens. <!-- ext:theme-i --> |

<!-- ext codes: the lines above tagged ext:<unit> are generated by scripts/ext/gen.mjs from bridge/ext/<unit>.md -->

## Events (frame to host): `{ type: string, ... }`
Each event carries exactly the keys listed here.

- `ev.state { phase: string, detail?: string }`; `phase` is one of `preflight`, `booting`, `staging`, `opening`, `blocked`, `fatal`, `popup`. `detail`, when present, is one of the island's own short strings, never engine or loader text:
  - `blocked`: the browser cannot run the engine; `detail` lists the failed capability codes, space separated (`no-sab`, `no-wasm`, `no-threads`, `no-webgl2`).
  - `fatal`: the engine stopped and the frame shows its fatal screen; `detail` is one of `memory` (out of memory), `crash` (the engine aborted; an import whose call trapped sends no `fatal`, its `import_failed` answer says so, `project.import` above), `boot_failed`, `engine_timeout` (no editor window within the boot time limit), `no_container`, `webgl_lost` (the browser lost the editor's WebGL context, for example on a GPU reset). The host reloads the frame to start again.
  - `popup` is informational: `ev.state { phase: "popup", detail: "<n> popup attempts blocked" }` may arrive at any time after `ev.ready`, once for every blocked popup attempt (`<n>` is the running count). It never changes the host's state. Attempts made before `ev.ready` are reported once, as a `booting` detail in the same words.
  - `booting`: while the engine's files load, `booting` repeats at most once every 2 s with `detail` the whole percent loaded, digits only (`0` to `100`), so a host that bounds the boot by inactivity sees it is still moving. Neither the first nor the 100 percent tick is promised.
  - `staging` and `opening` mark a `project.open` or a `project.import` in progress.
- `ev.ready { caps: string[], engine: { tag: string, kicad: string } }` once, after the engine booted and its editor window is up, and before any `project.open`: the host opens its project after this event. `caps` are the engine export names found on `Module`, sorted, and the pseudo-cap `picker` exactly when the fast part picker is live (`lib.index`, `lib.item`, `lib.prefetch`, `place` and `ev.pick`): the island's library mirror loaded and the engine has `kicadPlaceImportedItem` and `kicadCollabGetSelection`. On the example library (no mirror) `picker` is absent, the place keys stay KiCad's and no `ev.pick` is sent. A host gates its picker on `picker` (with `kicadPlaceImportedItem`), never on the export alone, which older islands list too. The pseudo-cap `selection` (island `v0.2.3-cc9`) is listed exactly when selection runs both ways: the engine has the selection hook for `ev.selected` (its `kicadCollabPresenceStart`) and `select` has its exports; a host gates both on it, never on the exports, which older islands list too. The pseudo-cap `write` (island `v0.2.3-cc10`) is listed by every island that answers `project.write` (it needs nothing of the engine but its file system); a host gates its writes on it. The pseudo-cap `fields` (island `v0.2.3-cc11`) is listed exactly when `picker` is, by islands whose `place` takes `value` and `footprint`; a host sends them only under it. The pseudo-cap `identity` (island `v0.2.3-cc11`) is listed exactly when `picker` is, by islands whose `place` takes `mpn` and `manufacturer`; a host sends them only under it (a local cc11 build of the morning of 2026-10-26 lists `fields` and refuses them). The pseudo-cap `projectLibs` (island `v0.2.3-cc12`) is listed when `project.libs` answers: the engine has `kicadLibsAddEntry`, `kicadLibsReload` and `kicadCollabBusy` (every engine since PCBJam v0.2.3 has them, so the word, never the exports, says the island takes the op). The pseudo-cap `models` (island `v0.2.3-cc12`) is listed when `footprint.models` answers: the engine has `kicadFootprintSetModels` (our engine build from pcbjam `cc/main` 466a85a on), `kicadCollabTestItemBlob` and `kicadCollabBusy`, in either frame's kind (a `sch` frame answers `unsupported`); a host gates the op on it. The pseudo-cap `plot` (island `v0.2.3-cc13`) is listed when `project.plot` answers: the engine has `kicadPlotBoard` (our engine build from pcbjam `cc/main` 924d67c on) and `kicadCollabBusy`, in either frame's kind (a `sch` frame answers `unsupported`); a host gates the op on it. The pseudo-caps `symbol`, `annotate` and `check` (island `v0.2.3-cc15`) are listed when `symbol.get` and `symbol.set`, `sheet.annotate` and `sheet.check` answer: the engine has `kicadSymbolGet` and `kicadSymbolSet`, `kicadAnnotate`, and `kicadCheckSheet` (our engine build from pcbjam `cc/main` e8b2abe on, engine run 10), each with `kicadCollabBusy`, in either frame's kind (a `pcb` frame answers `unsupported`); a host gates each on its word. The pseudo-cap `properties` (island `v0.2.3-cc16`) is listed by a `sch` frame exactly when its engine has `kicadHostProperties` (our engine build from pcbjam `cc/main` b1b5635 on, engine run 11): its schematic editor asks the frame before KiCad's symbol properties dialog opens, and the frame sends `ev.properties` instead (below); a `pcb` frame never lists it. A host that lists its own properties window gates it on this word. The pseudo-cap `hostKeys` (island `v0.2.3-cc17`) is listed by every frame that answers `keys.claim` and sends `ev.key`: they need nothing of the engine. A host gates its claim on this word. The pseudo-cap `observeKeys` (the island of engine build T17) is listed beside it by every frame whose `keys.claim` takes `observe` (it needs nothing of the engine either); a host sends `observe` only under it. The pseudo-caps `boardUpdate`, `drc`, `explain` and `boardItem` (island `v0.2.3-cc19`) are listed when `board.preview` and `board.update`, `board.check`, `board.explain`, and `board.item.get` and `board.item.set` answer: the engine has `kicadUpdateBoardFromSchematic` and `kicadCollabBusy`, `kicadCheckBoard` and `kicadCollabBusy`, `kicadExplainViolation`, and `kicadBoardItemGet`, `kicadBoardItemSet` and `kicadCollabBusy` (our engine from run 12), in either frame's kind (a `sch` frame answers them `unsupported`). `boardProperties` is listed when the engine has `kicadHostBoardProperties` (a `pcb` frame then sends `ev.boardProperties`), and `heldKeys` when it has `kicadHostActions` (the frame then sends `ev.held`), each in either frame's kind. A host gates each on its word. Island `v0.2.3-cc23` lists caps per frame: a `symedit` frame lists the engine's export names and only `symedit` (every symedit export, above), `symeditHeld` (the marker `kicadHostSymEdit`: `ev.symedit`), `symbolConvert` (`kicadSymbolLibConvert`), `heldKeys`, `hostKeys`, `write` and `projectLibs`, never a document frame's pseudo-cap (no `picker`, `selection`, `plot`, `symbol`, `boardItem`, ...: the merged engine carries every export, and each of those resolves another frame); a `sch` frame adds `symbolEdit` (`kicadSymbolForEditor`, `kicadSymbolPushToSchematic`, `kicadSymbolGet`, `kicadCollabBusy`), `symbolConvert` and `libUpdate` (`kicadUpdateFromLibrary`, `kicadCollabBusy`) to its list; a `pcb` frame lists none of the five. A `sch` frame also lists `simModel` (`kicadSimModelCatalog`, `kicadSimModelGet`, `kicadSimModelSet`, `kicadSimFileRead`, `kicadCollabBusy`) and `sim` (`kicadSimNetlist`, `kicadSimRun`, `kicadSimStop`, `kicadSimOpOnSheet`, `kicadCollabBusy`, and the simulator service served by the release); a `pcb` or `symedit` frame lists neither (The simulator, above). The pseudo-caps `export` and `rules` (island `v0.2.3-cc23`) are listed when `project.export` and `board.rules` answer: `export` when the engine has the frame's own kind's export (`kicadExportBoardOutput` in a `pcb` frame, `kicadExportSchematicOutput` in a `sch` frame) and `kicadCollabBusy`, `rules` when it has `kicadResolveRules`, in either document frame's kind (a `sch` frame answers `board.rules` `unsupported`); a `symedit` frame lists neither. A host gates each on its word, and an output on `island.json` `exportKinds` listing its job. The pseudo-caps of properties, numbering and rules (island `v0.2.3-cc23`) are listed by each document frame for its own kind only, each when the engine has its exports and `kicadCollabBusy`: a `sch` frame `symbols` (`kicadSymbolsGet`, `kicadSymbolsSet`), `symbolExchange` (`kicadSymbolsExchange`) and `schEdit` (`kicadSchEdit`), a `pcb` frame `boardFootprints` (`kicadBoardFootprints`), `boardEdit` (`kicadBoardEdit`) and `footprintExchange` (`kicadFootprintsExchange`), either `settings` (`kicadSettingsGet`, `kicadSettingsSet`); a `symedit` frame lists none. `engine.tag` is the PCBJam release tag the engine was built from; `engine.kicad` is the KiCad version string (for example `10.0`), not a commit.
- `ev.saved { path: string, bytes: Uint8Array }` after every save of a file in the project folder, whether the user pressed Ctrl+S or the host sent `project.save` (or `project.import`, for its converted board). One Ctrl+S, and one `project.save` in a schematic, emits several (each sheet file of a schematic and its `.kicad_pro`; a board's Ctrl+S, the `.kicad_pro` beside the board). A file saved outside the project folder (a Save As elsewhere) is not reported. None is emitted after `project.forget` until the next successful `project.open`. A `settings.set` of `customRules` that wrote the board's `.kicad_dru` (no dry run) sends it too, before its answer (island `v0.2.3-cc23`).
- `ev.openTool { frame: "sch" | "pcb" }` when the editor's own Switch or Quit menu asks for the other frame; the frame does not navigate.
- `ev.help { topic: string }` when the editor asked to open a KiCad help page; `topic` is the last path segment of the KiCad docs URL, `[a-z0-9_-]{1,64}`.
- `ev.menu { open: boolean }` when something of KiCad's own is drawn over its canvas: `open: true` when a popup menu (a context menu, KiCad's clarify-selection menu) or any dialog (a wx type whose name contains `Dialog`, progress dialogs included: an import's dialogs report it too) shows while none was up, `open: false` when the last of them closes. A host hides what it draws over the frame while it is open, so the menu or dialog is not covered. A popup menu is reported at once, a dialog within about 100 ms. Nothing is sent while nothing is up, nor after `shutdown` was answered or the frame went away.
- `ev.chrome { shown: boolean }` with KiCad's own chrome as the engine reports it: `shown: true` while its menu bar, its status bar or any pane but the canvas shows, `false` while none does. Sent once after `ev.ready` with the state the boot left (hidden), then each time it flips, which with the chrome hidden happens only through `chrome.show`: the engine holds the hidden chrome (a pane toggle such as Ctrl+H, a pane's close button, a save, a load or a theme change re-hides at once). A host renders its menus control from this event, never from its own toggle alone. Only from an engine with `kicadChromeShown` (our engine from run 9, pcbjam `cc/main` 27d82c2); an older one sends none. Nothing is sent after `shutdown` was answered or the frame went away.
- `ev.edited { depth: number }` when the open document's undo depth changes. The frame reads KiCad's undo command count every 500 ms while a document is open (from a successful `project.open` or `project.import` until `project.forget` or the next open), no dialog is up (progress dialogs included), no load is parked and no request is in flight (from its arrival to its answer, the whole of a `project.import` included), and right after each request's answer (even with more requests queued: the next has not started), and sends it when it differs from the last value read. `depth` is a whole number of at least 0: an edit raises it, an undo lowers it, a redo raises it again. A host reads any `ev.edited` as "the document changed" and never as a promise that unsaved changes exist: KiCad's undo list stops growing at its limit, and a save leaves it as it is. Each read is compared with the last one only, so an undo followed by a new edit inside one read interval leaves the depth unchanged and is not sent, as is an edit made once the list has stopped growing: a host keeps a periodic save as well. The first value read after an open or an import is the baseline and is not sent (opening a document is no edit); when the open answers while its load is still parked on a KiCad dialog (the file-version confirm on an older file), the baseline is the first value read once that load has settled, never one read while it was parked; a change made while a request was in flight is sent after that request's answer, so a host whose requests follow each other with no gap hears an undo during one and an edit back to the same depth during the next as two values. An engine without the `kicadCollabTestUndoDepth` export (absent from `ev.ready` `caps`) sends none, and nothing is sent after `shutdown` was answered or the frame went away. Added in island `v0.2.3-cc6`.
- `ev.pick { kind: "symbol" | "footprint", power?: true }` when the reader pressed KiCad's place key inside the frame: `A` in a `sch` frame (`kind: "symbol"`), `P` in a `sch` frame (`kind: "symbol", power: true`), `A` in a `pcb` frame (`kind: "footprint"`). The key counts by the character it types, in either case, with no Ctrl, Alt, Shift or Meta held, only while nothing of KiCad's own is over its canvas (no popup menu, no dialog but a progress dialog, no other window of KiCad's such as its footprint chooser), no text field has the keyboard focus and no `project.import` runs, and only when `ev.ready` listed `picker` (without the mirror the keys are KiCad's, as before). The frame takes the key from KiCad (its keydown, keypress and keyup), so KiCad's chooser never opens, and sends one `ev.pick` per press (a held key's repeats send none); the host opens its own picker and places with `place`. A `key.press` of those keys from the host does the same. `power` is present only when true. KiCad's own choosers stay reachable through its menus (`chrome.show`). Added in island `v0.2.3-cc7`.
- `ev.selected { uuids: string[], fpPaths: string[] }` when the editor's selection changed and has settled: `uuids` are the selected items' uuids in KiCad's order, `fpPaths` each selected footprint's schematic path (`/<sheet uuid>/.../<symbol uuid>`, a board only: always `[]` in a `sch` frame, and a footprint with no path has none); an emptied selection sends `{ uuids: [], fpPaths: [] }`. It comes from the engine's own selection hook (`window.kicadCollab.onSelection`, armed once through `kicadCollabPresenceStart`: at `ev.ready` in a `pcb` frame, at the first successful `project.open` or `project.import` in a `sch` frame, for the engine's collab document listener the call registers; `bridge/selection.ts` says why), which the engine fires after each press, key or wheel in the drawing and each change to the document has been handled, and from reads of the selection about 0, 200 and 600 ms after each pointer or key release in the frame (a selection made in KiCad's clarify-selection menu raises no hook). A burst is sent once, about 150 ms after it settled, and only when it differs from the last selection sent; a `select` the host sent arrives the same way. Sent only while a document is open (from a successful `project.open` or `project.import` until `project.forget` or the next open; nothing while an import runs), and every document starts with an empty selection, which is never sent: the first `ev.selected` after an open names a selection. Only when `ev.ready` listed `selection`. Added in island `v0.2.3-cc9`.
- `ev.properties { uuids: string[], sheetPath: string }` when the reader asked for a symbol's properties in a `sch` frame: a double-click on the symbol, its `E` key over it or its context menu's Properties, on the symbol's body, one of its pins or one of its fields (a lone pin or field counts as its symbol). `uuids` holds exactly the symbol's uuid; `sheetPath` is the sheet it sits on in `project.open`'s form (`/` for the root, then one uuid and a slash per level), the form `symbol.set` requires and `sheet.enter` takes. KiCad's symbol properties dialog does not open, and the symbol stays the one selected, so `ev.selected` names the same uuid (a pin or field selection gives way to its symbol). Sent only while a document is open (from a successful `project.open` or `project.import` until `project.forget` or the next open), after `ev.ready`, and never after `shutdown` was answered; outside that, and for every other item (a label, a wire, a sheet, text), KiCad's own dialog opens as before and `ev.menu` reports it. Only from an engine with `kicadHostProperties` (our engine from run 11: kicad `cc/main` ffe73d65, the hook in `SCH_EDIT_TOOL::Properties`, pinned by pcbjam `cc/main` b1b5635; island `v0.2.3-cc16`), where the frame lists the `properties` cap; an older one sends none and KiCad's dialog opens. `field` (island `v0.2.3-cc19`), 1 to 128 characters, is present only when KiCad named one of the symbol's fields: the U, V and F keys over a symbol (Edit Reference, Value, Footprint; our engine from run 12 asks first there too, `editFieldText`) send `Reference`, `Value` or `Footprint`, and a lone field selected sends its own name; the host opens its form on that field. A name past 128 characters is left out and the symbol taken all the same.
- `ev.boardProperties { uuid: string, kind: "footprint" | "track" | "arc" | "via" | "zone" | "text" | "marker" }` (island `v0.2.3-cc19`, the `boardProperties` cap) when the reader asked for one board item's properties in a `pcb` frame: a double-click on it, its `E` key or its context menu's Properties, on one footprint (a field or text of it counts as its footprint), track, arc, via, copper area (not a keep out area), board text or a check's mark. KiCad's dialog does not open (for a mark, KiCad's design rules checker) and the item stays the one selected, so an `ev.selected` naming it follows when the selection changed. Sent only while a document is open, after `ev.ready`, never after `shutdown` was answered; anything else (a pad, a drawn shape, a dimension, several items) opens KiCad's own dialog as before. Only from an engine with `kicadHostBoardProperties` (our engine from run 12: the hook in `EDIT_TOOL::Properties`).

<!-- ext events: the lines above tagged ext:<unit> are generated by scripts/ext/gen.mjs from bridge/ext/<unit>.md -->

Unknown fields are rejected by both sides. An unknown event `type` is ignored by the host; an unknown `op` is answered `unknown_op` (a manifest op whose unit has not landed: `unsupported`, see `unknown_op` above).

## Leaving the frame
KiCad asks "Leave site?" when the frame unloads with unsaved edits. The frame keeps that prompt quiet when the host is about to reload it and nothing would be lost:
- for 10 s after a `project.save` that emitted `ev.saved`, or a successful `project.import` (the host has the bytes);
- from `project.forget` until the next successful `project.open`, whatever the forget answered (`island_error` included).

A Ctrl+S in the editor does not quiet it, and outside these windows a reload with unsaved edits still gets the prompt.

## island.json
Each island release serves `island.json` beside its page, and the island host serves the current one at `/island.json`:

| field | meaning |
|---|---|
| `id` | the islandId, `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9.-]{0,63}$`; the release's path is `/r/<id>/` and its source is the release tagged `<id>` |
| `tag` | the PCBJam release tag the engine was built from (the same value as `ev.ready` `engine.tag`) |
| `kicadCommit` | the KiCad source commit the engine was built from (a full SHA), not a version string |
| `source` | the address of this release's source |
| `ops` | the request ops this release answers, sorted (island `v0.2.3-cc13`; absent from older releases): a host learns from it that a release answers `project.plot` before any engine boots, and treats an absent `ops` as answering none of the ops added since |
| `exportKinds` | the jobs `project.export` runs, sorted (island `v0.2.3-cc23`, from `bridge/exports.json`; absent from older releases): a host shows an output whose job is not listed as one this island cannot make, and treats an absent `exportKinds` as listing none |

A host needs only `id`.

## Versioning
`proto` is the major. A host that sees a major it does not know ignores the hello. New ops, events, error codes and `island.json` fields are added within a major; removing or changing one bumps it. `ev.edited` (island `v0.2.3-cc6`) is such an addition: a host that does not know it ignores it, and a host facing an older island never receives it. So are `ev.pick`, `lib.index`, `lib.item`, `lib.prefetch`, `place` and `not_found`, with one change an older host does meet: the island takes KiCad's place keys (`A`, and `P` in a schematic) for `ev.pick`, which an older host ignores, so the reader's `A` does nothing there. The host that handles `ev.pick` ships before the island that sends it; it finds an older island by the absent `picker` cap and keeps pressing KiCad's keys for it. `ev.selected` and `select` (island `v0.2.3-cc9`) are additions too: a host finds an older island by the absent `selection` cap. `project.write`, the error code `open_document` and the `write` cap arrive with island `v0.2.3-cc10`: a host finds an older island by the absent `write` cap. `place`'s `value` and `footprint` and the `fields` cap arrive with island `v0.2.3-cc11`: a host finds an older island by the absent `fields` cap. So do `place`'s `mpn` and `manufacturer` and the `identity` cap: a host finds an island without them by the absent `identity` cap. `project.libs` and the `projectLibs` cap, and `footprint.models` and the `models` cap, arrive with island `v0.2.3-cc12`: a host finds an older island by the absent cap, and an island whose engine lacks `kicadFootprintSetModels` by the absent `models` cap. `project.plot`, the error code `plot_failed`, the `plot` cap and `island.json` `ops` arrive with island `v0.2.3-cc13`: a host finds an older island by the absent `plot` cap, or before any engine boots by an `island.json` whose `ops` is absent or does not list `project.plot`. `symbol.get`, `symbol.set`, `sheet.annotate`, `sheet.check`, the `symbol`, `annotate` and `check` caps, and `not_found` for a symbol arrive with island `v0.2.3-cc15`: a host finds an older island by the absent cap, or before any engine boots by an `island.json` `ops` without the op. `ev.properties` and the `properties` cap arrive with island `v0.2.3-cc16`: a host finds an older island, or a board frame, by the absent `properties` cap, and there KiCad's symbol properties dialog still opens. `keys.claim`, `ev.key` and the `hostKeys` cap arrive with island `v0.2.3-cc17`: an older island lists no `hostKeys` and answers `keys.claim` `unknown_op`, so a host's Alt chords reach KiCad there while the frame has the focus. `board.preview`, `board.update`, `board.check`, `board.explain`, `board.item.get`, `board.item.set`, `ev.boardProperties`, `ev.held`, `ev.properties`'s `field`, the codes `no_schematic`, `schematic_unreadable`, `stale`, `not_annotated`, `rules_invalid` and `no_rule`, and the caps `boardUpdate`, `drc`, `explain`, `boardItem`, `boardProperties` and `heldKeys` arrive with island `v0.2.3-cc19` (`v0.2.3-cc18` carried them on engine run 12 as first built, before its connectivity repair, and was never released): a host finds an older island, or an island on an older engine, by the absent cap, or before any engine boots by an `island.json` `ops` without the op; there F8, the board check's key and the panes' keys open KiCad's windows as before, and a host that does not know `field` reads the symbol whole. Island `v0.2.3-cc22` adds and removes nothing: `project.open` and `project.import` answer without the 1.5 s chrome watch that used to precede every answer, so their `chrome` says what is visible at the answer, after the frame's one hide, where it said what was visible after the watch. A host reads it as before (`true`: send `chrome.show { on: false }` again) and learns of a later flip from `ev.chrome`. Every time this document measured before `v0.2.3-cc22` for an open or an import includes that 1.5 s. Island `v0.2.3-cc23` adds the `symedit` frame, the eighteen ops `symedit.open`, `symedit.new`, `symedit.state`, `symedit.take`, `symedit.markSaved`, `symedit.items.get`, `symedit.items.set`, `symedit.items.add`, `symedit.items.remove`, `symedit.symbol.get`, `symedit.symbol.set`, `symedit.show`, `symedit.check`, `symedit.svg`, `symbol.convert`, `symbol.forEditor`, `symbol.push` and `lib.update`, `ev.symedit`, the codes `held_edits`, `no_symbol`, `unreadable` and `too_large`, the caps `symedit`, `symeditHeld`, `symbolConvert`, `symbolEdit` and `libUpdate`, and the held actions above: a host finds an older island by the absent cap, or before any engine boots by an `island.json` `ops` without the op (an older island boots a `symedit` frame as a `sch` frame). One change an older host meets: a `sch` frame now holds Ctrl+E (Edit with Symbol Editor) under `symbolEdit`, so KiCad's symbol editor window never opens there. Island `v0.2.3-cc23` also adds the simulator: the eleven ops `sim.catalog`, `sim.model.get`, `sim.model.set`, `sim.file`, `sim.netlist`, `sim.run`, `sim.result`, `sim.vectors`, `sim.stop`, `sim.opOnSheet` and `sim.release`, `ev.sim`, the codes `no_directive`, `no_simulation`, `sim_failed`, `circuit`, `service_failed` and `model_invalid`, the caps `sim` and `simModel`, and the held `eeschema.EditorControl.showSimulator`: a host finds an older island by the absent cap, or before any engine boots by an `island.json` `ops` without the op. One change an older host meets: KiCad's Inspect > Simulator in a `sch` frame now sends `ev.held` and opens no window. `project.export`, `board.rules`, the code `export_failed`, `not_found`, `rules_invalid` and `no_rule` for `board.rules`, the caps `export` and `rules`, and `island.json` `exportKinds` arrive with `v0.2.3-cc23` as well: a host finds an older island by the absent cap, or before any engine boots by an `island.json` `ops` without the op, and an output this island does not make by `exportKinds`. `symbols.get`, `symbols.set`, `symbols.exchange`, `sch.edit`, `board.footprints`, `board.edit`, `footprints.exchange`, `settings.get` and `settings.set`, the codes `no_variant` and `duplicate_designator` (and `not_found`, `rules_invalid`, `bad_args` and `unsupported` for them), `ev.saved` of a custom rules file, and the caps `symbols`, `symbolExchange`, `schEdit`, `boardFootprints`, `boardEdit`, `footprintExchange` and `settings` arrive with `v0.2.3-cc23` as well: a host finds an older island by the absent cap, or before any engine boots by an `island.json` `ops` without the op. The island of engine build T17 adds `libs.problems`, `actions.state`, `ev.tool`, `ev.fpeditNotice`, `keys.claim`'s `observe` and `ev.key`'s `observed`, and the caps `libTableProblems`, `actionStates`, `menuClarify`, `activeTool`, `fpeditNotice` and `observeKeys`; `board.vertices.set` waits for the queued move (same answer), and `ev.contextMenu` `clarify` is exact under `menuClarify`: a host finds an older island, or an island on an older engine, by the absent cap, or before any engine boots by an `island.json` `ops` without the op.

The islandId is also the cache key: `/r/<id>/` is served `immutable`, and the loader checks no engine byte. So an engine is never rebuilt under an islandId that has been released or shipped: a new engine build after a release or a ship takes a new islandId. A local engine swap under the same id (`fetch-engine --local` and `npm run build`, as runs 4 and 5 both served `v0.2.3-cc8` before any release) is tested only after the editor origin's cache is cleared (its site data, or a fresh browser profile), and the frame's console line `[build] wasm kicad_editor/v0.2.3/kicad_editor.wasm etag=<...> head128k-sha256=<first 24 hex of the sha256 of the wasm's first 128 KiB> streamed=<bytes on the wire>B` proves which bytes ran: engine run 5 prints `head128k-sha256=75d7ed733cca05d0855cb1ee streamed=16662766B` (the gzip body both the local pair's nginx and `tests/serve.mjs` send; run 4 printed `177b858177f01840ef73d12b` and `16662560B`).
